Title: An Account of W. Penn's Travails in Holland and Germany, Anno MDCLXXVII, for the Service of the Gospel of Christ, by way of Journal; containing also divers Letters and Epistles writ to several Great and Eminent Persons whilst there
Creator: William Penn (1644-1718)
Publisher: London: printed and sold by T. Sowle near the Meeting-House in White-Hart-Court in Gracechurch Street, and at the Crooked-Billet in Holywell Lane near Shoreditch
Publication Date: 1694
Rights: Public Domain (published 1694, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item accountofwpennst00penn (https://archive.org/details/accountofwpennst00penn), the item's own accountofwpennst00penn_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: University of Pittsburgh Library System; host metadata date: 1694. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 3b205a06593104d747c38c6b2a754af5ab507fda78b6127339e9a7712a5529ab.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. University of Pittsburgh scan; readable with the long s rendered as f by the OCR; a library plate precedes the title page and is left in place.
Content note: Penn's journal of his 1677 journey with Fox, Barclay and Keith through Holland and the Rhineland, with his letters to Princess Elizabeth of the Palatinate and others; the Quaker mission to the continental Pietists in the traveller's own record.
